# CORE-CAP-SPECIALTY — consolidated source profiles

Uses an existing registered canonical owner and preserves all prior addenda. For Pharmacy this is the first canonical specification under the already registered owner, not a new architecture. Every clause below applies only to its listed source profiles; it is not a universal clinical rule. No new service, clinical approval or implementation readiness.

## disease-workflows

Existing Specialty owns the domain workflow and longitudinal projection only. Condition, laboratory, imaging, medication, prescription, encounter and device owners retain authoritative facts and actions. Source-specific disease names, severity scales, treatment pathways, age, phenotype and specialty context remain independent profile variants, not interchangeable clinical protocols.

Acceptance additions: Retain suspected/confirmed/unknown states, source versions, timestamps and clinician attribution. Test contradictory histories and amended results without silently creating diagnoses. Pediatric guardian access and confidential adolescent segments are separately authorized. Dosing, device commands, surgery, crisis disposition and treatment eligibility require qualified clinical decisions and validated intended-use criteria; source automation claims are not execution authority.

## care-and-transition

Existing Specialty owns the domain workflow and longitudinal projection only. Condition, laboratory, imaging, medication, prescription, encounter and device owners retain authoritative facts and actions. Source-specific disease names, severity scales, treatment pathways, age, phenotype and specialty context remain independent profile variants, not interchangeable clinical protocols. Surgical plans versus completed procedures, rehabilitation goals versus device settings, and referrals versus accepted handovers are distinct. Home care, remote sessions and Twin representations delegate to their existing owners; shared-care views never grant school/family access implicitly.

Acceptance additions: Retain suspected/confirmed/unknown states, source versions, timestamps and clinician attribution. Test contradictory histories and amended results without silently creating diagnoses. Pediatric guardian access and confidential adolescent segments are separately authorized. Dosing, device commands, surgery, crisis disposition and treatment eligibility require qualified clinical decisions and validated intended-use criteria; source automation claims are not execution authority. Preserve author, recipient, pending/accepted handover and revocation at age/authority transitions. Automated rehabilitation adjustment remains a proposal requiring clinician approval.

Existing Specialty owns the domain workflow and longitudinal projection only. ## management-workflows

Existing Specialty owns the domain workflow and longitudinal projection only. Condition, laboratory, imaging, medication, prescription, encounter and device owners retain authoritative facts and actions. Source-specific disease names, severity scales, treatment pathways, age, phenotype and specialty context remain independent profile variants, not interchangeable clinical protocols. Management is care coordination, not autonomous therapy. Preserve nutrition, renal access, infusion, mood/crisis, developmental and sensitive-examination requirements as scoped variants.

Acceptance additions: Retain suspected/confirmed/unknown states, source versions, timestamps and clinician attribution. Test contradictory histories and amended results without silently creating diagnoses. Pediatric guardian access and confidential adolescent segments are separately authorized. Dosing, device commands, surgery, crisis disposition and treatment eligibility require qualified clinical decisions and validated intended-use criteria; source automation claims are not execution authority.

## condition-variants

Existing Specialty owns the domain workflow and longitudinal projection only. Condition, laboratory, imaging, medication, prescription, encounter and device owners retain authoritative facts and actions. Source-specific disease names, severity scales, treatment pathways, age, phenotype and specialty context remain independent profile variants, not interchangeable clinical protocols. Infection and cancer variants preserve organism/site/exposure and tumor biology/stage; no negative screen can silently exclude urgent disease.

Acceptance additions: Retain suspected/confirmed/unknown states, source versions, timestamps and clinician attribution. Test contradictory histories and amended results without silently creating diagnoses. Pediatric guardian access and confidential adolescent segments are separately authorized. Dosing, device commands, surgery, crisis disposition and treatment eligibility require qualified clinical decisions and validated intended-use criteria; source automation claims are not execution authority.

## genetics-workflows

Existing Specialty owns the domain workflow and longitudinal projection only. Condition, laboratory, imaging, medication, prescription, encounter and device owners retain authoritative facts and actions. Source-specific disease names, severity scales, treatment pathways, age, phenotype and specialty context remain independent profile variants, not interchangeable clinical protocols. Genetics profiles coordinate phenotype, pedigree, counseling, testing and longitudinal follow-up. Laboratory owns assay/variant reports; Interop owns coded exchange. Do not merge germline, somatic, prenatal, carrier and research interpretations or treat relatives as the same patient.

Acceptance additions: Retain suspected/confirmed/unknown states, source versions, timestamps and clinician attribution. Test contradictory histories and amended results without silently creating diagnoses. Pediatric guardian access and confidential adolescent segments are separately authorized. Dosing, device commands, surgery, crisis disposition and treatment eligibility require qualified clinical decisions and validated intended-use criteria; source automation claims are not execution authority. Preserve assay/assembly/transcript/interpretation version and uncertainty, family-member-specific permission, incidental-finding preferences and reclassification. Variant presence is not diagnosis or therapy eligibility.

## additional-specialty-pathways

Existing Specialty owns the domain workflow and longitudinal projection only. Condition, laboratory, imaging, medication, prescription, encounter and device owners retain authoritative facts and actions. Source-specific disease names, severity scales, treatment pathways, age, phenotype and specialty context remain independent profile variants, not interchangeable clinical protocols. Transplant eligibility, oncologic staging and precision predictions remain specialist-reviewed. Robotic urology is a clinical workflow profile and cannot issue robot commands; device ownership remains separate.

Acceptance additions: Retain suspected/confirmed/unknown states, source versions, timestamps and clinician attribution. Test contradictory histories and amended results without silently creating diagnoses. Pediatric guardian access and confidential adolescent segments are separately authorized. Dosing, device commands, surgery, crisis disposition and treatment eligibility require qualified clinical decisions and validated intended-use criteria; source automation claims are not execution authority. Preserve donor/recipient identity separation, immunosuppression exposure, developmental stage and tumor-specific evidence. No common title establishes an equivalent protocol.

## additional-condition-pathways

Existing Specialty owns the domain workflow and longitudinal projection only. Condition, laboratory, imaging, medication, prescription, encounter and device owners retain authoritative facts and actions. Source-specific disease names, severity scales, treatment pathways, age, phenotype and specialty context remain independent profile variants, not interchangeable clinical protocols. Screening, assessment and diagnosis have separate statuses. Preserve instrument/version and clinician interpretation, nutrition/growth trajectories, privacy-sensitive mental-health and reproductive context; no score or screening result alone authorizes clinical disposition.

Acceptance additions: Retain suspected/confirmed/unknown states, source versions, timestamps and clinician attribution. Test contradictory histories and amended results without silently creating diagnoses. Pediatric guardian access and confidential adolescent segments are separately authorized. Dosing, device commands, surgery, crisis disposition and treatment eligibility require qualified clinical decisions and validated intended-use criteria; source automation claims are not execution authority. Distinguish not screened, negative screen, incomplete screen and confirmed disease. Missing red flags or reassuring scores cannot exclude urgent illness; assessment and crisis escalation need separate intended-use validation.

## clinical-integration-workflows

Existing Specialty owns the domain workflow and longitudinal projection only. Condition, laboratory, imaging, medication, prescription, encounter and device owners retain authoritative facts and actions. Source-specific disease names, severity scales, treatment pathways, age, phenotype and specialty context remain independent profile variants, not interchangeable clinical protocols. Integration here means coordinated clinical workflows and linked views, not a new exchange service. Interop retains transport/terminology ownership; pathology, imaging, prescriptions, devices, vaccination and inventory remain authoritative in their own domains. Cross-specialty timelines retain each author and patient binding.

Acceptance additions: Retain suspected/confirmed/unknown states, source versions, timestamps and clinician attribution. Test contradictory histories and amended results without silently creating diagnoses. Pediatric guardian access and confidential adolescent segments are separately authorized. Dosing, device commands, surgery, crisis disposition and treatment eligibility require qualified clinical decisions and validated intended-use criteria; source automation claims are not execution authority. Test differing timestamps, unresolved identity, revoked permissions and contradictory specialty conclusions. Vaccination-associated rash is not automatically allergy; occupational status views disclose only approved minimum fields. Clinical integration cannot broaden parent/guardian/school access.
